Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the algebraic_geom structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 603. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 876 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 603 + O(1/n^876). Summing over all modes and applying the algebraic_geom convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
